# 原作定义目录 / Original definition catalog

来源为原作 SHIPDEFS_DAT 的已提取数值字段；不是完整原作者源码或设计稿。全部 387 个定义保留，包含未使用定义。 / Extracted numeric fields from SHIPDEFS_DAT, not original source code or an authored design document. All 387 definitions are retained, including unused ones.

路径节点为原始相对数据，首节点及 mode 10 不能一概累加，见 [时序与坐标规则](../RULES.md)。炮口数组保留原始 18 个整数；延迟是计数，不是保证在关卡第几秒开火。 / Paths are raw relative data; first nodes and mode 10 require the linked rules. Gun arrays preserve all eighteen integers; delays are counters, not guaranteed absolute firing times.
